NO accounting allegation, NO regulator, NO advertiser boycott, NO short-seller involvement appears anywhere in this call.** 🔴🔴🔴 **NET-NEW #3, AND THE MOST IMPORTANT — A MARGIN MECHANISM NO SOURCE ON THIS PAGE HAD NAMED: *"For a long time AppLovin has been able to generate high CPMs from non-gaming advertisers, but pay out low CPMs to gaming publishers. But the more the competition steps up, the more we'll have to pay out."* The 84% adj. EBITDA margin Foroughi called a moat on 09-21 IS that spread; competition can compress it without taking any share. No size, no timing — a direction only.** 🔴 **NET-NEW #4 — A DIRECT CONTRADICTION OF THE MS EXPERT INGESTED 09-20 ON THIS PAGE'S CENTRAL COMPETITIVE QUESTION: Legg raised Unity (Q3 +27%→+31%, Q4 +23%→+28%, FY26 ~+25%→~+27%, Q1'27 +28%) and attributes part of it to *"AppLovin advertisers… worried about being displaced and therefore taking other alternatives more seriously"* — where MS's expert concluded *"Unity's recent growth has NOT come at APP's expense."* Both can hold today (MS measured realised wallet share at ONE firm; Legg measures intent across a panel) but they predict opposite outcomes over the next twelve months, which is the window MS's own expert dated the Unity runtime-data test to. Unity scale check: only 4 of Legg's 38 August Unity advertisers are non-gaming.** 🔽 **NET-NEW #5 — one bull talking point CLOSED: the social-creative-porting initiative delivers ZERO incremental unlock in his panel because his advertisers were already running optimised creatives.** ⚠️ **SUPERSEDED, OLD VALUES PRESERVED HERE PER THE THESIS-DRIFT RULE: (a) `## Catalysts` previously read "Whether META re-enters the non-IDFA mobile-ad market (**dormant, unrefuted**)" — "dormant" no longer holds: Meta's Audience Network went 2.1% → 2.6% of Legg's panel spend April→August after sitting at ~2.1% "for two or three years," and gaming was Meta's fastest-growing vertical in August at +89% y/y vs Meta overall +29%. Still NOT proof of a non-IDFA bid — but the surface such a bid would run through has broken a multi-year flat line. (b) `## Risks` previously described Unity/Vector as "**distinct from the dormant META bear**"; both are now live and are arguably one bear — a coordinated bid-up of the gaming auction.** ✅ **NOTHING ELSE SUPERSEDED — the rating ladder (MS $650 / DB $580 / GS $465 / JPM $400) and the Q3-print test are untouched; Legg attaches no rating, PT or estimate to anything. | 2026-09-21 | ⭐ BE | (/run-inbox, 23h window) — 1 source: Morgan Stanley · Stephen C Byrd / James E Faucette (with David Arcaro, Robert Kad, Andrew Percoco, Cameron McVeigh, Angel Castillo, Joe Laetsch, Joseph Moore), *"Powering AI: Racking Up the Power Shortfall"*, North America Insight, 2026-09-21. THIS IS THE PRIMARY BEHIND THE SALES RELAY THIS PAGE INGESTED AT 21h.** Added 1 `## Catalysts` bullet, 1 `## Risks` bullet, 1 new `Signal vs management` row, an extension to **Síntese do quarter**, 1 intra-quarter full-log row (09-21), 1 Sources bullet and this record. 🔴🔴🔴 **THIS IS THE LARGEST SUPERSESSION THIS PAGE HAS TAKEN, AND THE OLD VALUES ARE PRESERVED BOTH IN PLACE AND HERE. The MS "Power Struggle" (07-14) shortfall architecture that this page has carried as its sharpest bear argument since 08-20 — *"38 GW power bottleneck over 2026-28, down from our prior 47 GW for 2025-28"*; the waterfall **68 − 15 under construction − 15 grid access = 38**; remedies **nat-gas turbines 15/18/20 @90% · BLOOM 5/7/8 @90% · nuclear co-location −/3/5 @75% · bitcoin conversion 10/14/19 @90%** → **27/37/46 GW**, net **(11)/(1)/none = 16%/2%/0%**, with MS labelling the midpoint *"the most likely outcome"* — IS SUPERSEDED BY THE 09-21 PRIMARY:** **97 GW of 2026-28 US DC power demand − 21 GW of DCs under construction − 19 GW of utility grid access = 57 GW GROSS (*"a 50% increase from our previous estimate"*)**; remedies **BTM turbines and reciprocating engines 11 / 19 / 49 @90% · BLOOM ENERGY FUEL CELLS 5 / 6 / 8 @90% · site a DC at an operational nuclear plant 1 / 3 / 8 @75% · convert PSP sites 8 / 13 / 19 @90% BUT NOW EXPLICITLY EXCLUDED from the reduction** → probability-weighted **15 / 24 / 57 GW** → **NET (42) / (33) / (0) GW = 43% / 34% / 0%**, midpoint again *"most likely"*; extended to 2029, **165 GW of demand → 107 GW gross / 72-82 GW net = 44-49%**, annual cumulative net **5 / 12 / 33 / 72 GW** with the **Bloom line at 1 / 2 / 5 / 9 GW**. 🔴 **THE SIGN FLIPS AND THE BEAR MUTATES. The "gap is closing to ~1 GW by 2028" argument is retired by the house that created it. What replaces it is a RELATIVE-SHARE bear: Bloom's midpoint is CUT to 6 GW from 7 GW while BTM turbines and engines are raised to a 19 GW midpoint and a 49 GW high (from 20 GW), so on the high case MS models 49 GW of engines against 8 GW of fuel cells — a 6:1 ratio versus a prior 2.5:1. Nuclear co-siting, which 07-14 had cut to 0-5 GW in BE's favour, recovers to 1 / 3 / 8 GW at 75%.** ✅ **ARITHMETIC VERIFIED IN-HOUSE BEFORE ADOPTION, so the two exhibits can be used together: 19×0.9 + 6×0.9 + 3×0.75 = 24.75 ≈ MS's printed 24 GW midpoint; 11×0.9 + 5×0.9 + 1×0.75 = 15.15 ≈ 15; 49×0.9 + 8×0.9 + 8×0.75 = 57.3 ≈ 57; and 57 − 24 = 33. The apparent 6-vs-5 discrepancy between the Bloom bucket and the Bloom annual line is just the 90% weighting — not an inconsistency.** ⚠️⚠️ **GUARD CARRIED INTO THE BODY, AND IT IS THE THING THE 21h RELAY OMITS: MS discounts its own headline — *"part of the Net Shortfall increase was the elimination of a DOUBLE-COUNTING of PSP sites, artificially reducing the shortfall by ~15GW in the previous model."* Roughly half the 32 GW net move is an accounting correction to MS's own prior model. The genuine raise is on GROSS DEMAND, 68 → 97 GW, and that is the 8-GPU-server → NVL72 rack re-spec. Size the thesis off the demand raise, not off the net shortfall.** 🔴 **TWO FURTHER CORRECTIONS TO THE 21h RELAY ENTRY, RECORDED HERE AND NOT DELETED FROM IT: the note is titled *"Powering AI: Racking Up the Power Shortfall"*, not *"Power Shortfall Update"*; and the cover lead authors are **Stephen C Byrd and James E Faucette**, with Arcaro one of eight further contributors.** 🆕 **NET-NEW:** BE's own MS stock paragraph (*"one of the most direct solutions to the time-to-power bottleneck… secure onsite power BEFORE grid interconnections arrive… INTERCONNECTION TIMELINES EXTEND BEYOND FIVE YEARS"*), which is the permitting/speed moat this page already treats as its best-corroborated leg, now stated by MS in the same terms; BE named as one of **three US Overweights in power-generation equipment alongside GEV and CMI**; and the annual path of the shortfall, which this page has never had. ⚠️ **NOTHING ELSE SUPERSEDED — no BE rating, price target, estimate, guide, MW figure or valuation mark was replaced. This note carries NO BE rating, PT or estimate; UBS Buy / $325 (09-06) and the 07-14 DCF mechanics (unlevered DCF, 10.8% discount rate, 4.0% risk-free, 1.75 beta, 4.29% ERP, no PT) both stand.** 📌 **Cross-check logged, not adopted: @FundaAI's 09-04 ~15 GW North-American 2027 shortfall (no method shown) sits against MS's 12 GW annual cumulative net for 2027 — same order of magnitude, two independent third-party forecasts, neither adopted.** |

That SUPPORTS the headline conclusion (*"Unity's recent growth has NOT come at APP's expense"*, with Vector *"gaining share primarily from smaller networks"*), but it also means ~20 points went somewhere neither company is named in. Treat the 55%→30% and 1%→6% series as an anecdote with unusually good precision, not as market share.** Added: 1 dated section, 1 `## Sources` entry, this record. |
